  "old_string": "\\looseness=-1 Fine-tuning DeBERTa on ProofWriter depth-5 OWA (99.67\\% standalone) amplifies the escape: combo gains reach ${\\sim}$$+$13\\,pp (Mistral-7B, $w{=}3$) and ${\\sim}$$+$12\\,pp (LLaMA-8B), $2$--$3.5\\times$ the zero-shot result, with near-total error complementarity ($365{:}2$ and $164{:}1$ NLI-correct/SC-incorrect vs.\\ reverse; Table~\\ref{tab:finetuned-nli}, Appendix). A cost-aware cascade variant recovers 90\\% of full-combo gain at ${\\sim}$35\\% NLI cost (Appendix~\\ref{app:drc}).",
  "new_string": "\\looseness=-1 Fine-tuning DeBERTa on ProofWriter depth-5 OWA (99.67\\% standalone) amplifies the escape: combo gains reach ${\\sim}$$+$13\\,pp (Mistral-7B, $w{=}3$) and ${\\sim}$$+$12\\,pp (LLaMA-8B), $2$--$3.5\\times$ the zero-shot result, with near-total error complementarity ($365{:}2$ and $164{:}1$ NLI-correct/SC-incorrect vs.\\ reverse; Table~\\ref{tab:finetuned-nli}, Appendix). }

### Narrative 变化（核心）
LLaMA 不再是 "bridge case"。新叙事：
- **Zero-shot NLI 只对最弱 generator 有效**（Mistral SC≈39%，显著获益）
- **SC ≥ 72% 的 generator 不获益**（LLaMA/Qwen2.5/Qwen3 全部 n.s.）
- **Domain adaptation 是 escape 的关键**：FT DeBERTa 将 LLaMA 从 +0.83pp (n.s.) 提升到 +11.67pp (p<0.0001)，14× 放大
- 这是更 clean 的 story：零-shot 不够 → 需要 domain adaptation → 可操作建议

### Table 3 D061 精确数据（solutions.tex）

DeBERTa-large-mnli standalone: 55.00%

| Generator | Family | SC | Gap | Combo Δ (w=3) | p |
|-----------|--------|------|------|--------------|---|
| Mistral-7B | Mistral | 39.17% | +15.83 | +7.00pp | <0.001 |
| LLaMA-3.1-8B | LLaMA | 72.50% | -17.50 | +0.83pp | 0.665 |
| Qwen2.5-14B | Qwen | 74.00% | -19.00 | +0.67pp | 0.503 |
| Qwen3-14B | Qwen | 85.83% | -30.83 | +0.50pp | 0.45 |

### §6 "Main result" 段落重写要点
- 删除 "LLaMA gains +3.33pp (significant at w=1)"
- 改为 "significant gains only for Mistral (w≥3: +7.00pp, p<0.001; w≥5: +11.17pp)"
- LLaMA/Qwen "show no significant improvement"
- "After BH correction (m=12), two conditions remain significant" (was three)

### §6 "Capability gap" 段落重写要点
- LLaMA 不是 bridge —— SC≥72% 全部不获益
- Spearman ρ=1.0 仍成立（排序不变）
- "generators above SC≈55% show no improvement" (was ~74%)

### §6 "Domain-adapted verifier" 段落更新
- "Zero-shot NLI provides no significant benefit for generators above SC~55%"
- FT amplification: LLaMA zero-shot +0.83pp (n.s.) → FT +11.67pp (p<0.0001) = **14×**
- 删除 "~3.5×"，替换 "14×"
- 这反而强化了 domain adaptation 的必要性

1	\section{Cross-Architecture Proof-of-Concept}
2	\label{sec:solutions}
3	\label{sec:cross-arch}
4	
5	\paragraph{Method.}
6	\looseness=-1 We test with DeBERTa-large \citep{he2021deberta} (406M, encoder-only), fine-tuned on MultiNLI \citep{williams2018mnli}, classifying (premise, conclusion) pairs as entailment, contradiction, or neutral. In combo mode, each NLI-supporting vote counts $w$ times alongside SC's $K$ votes. We evaluate on Mistral-7B FOLIO ($n = 204$) and ProofWriter depth-5 ($n = 600$).
7	
8	\paragraph{Main result.}
9	\looseness=-1 DeBERTa NLI alone achieves 49.51\% on FOLIO (below SC's 55.88\%) and 55.00\% on ProofWriter (above SC's 39.33\%). On FOLIO ($n = 204$), combo $w{=}3$ yields $+2.94$\,pp (n.s.). On ProofWriter ($n{=}600$), combo $w{=}3$ achieves a significant gain for Mistral-7B ($+6.67$\,pp, $p < 0.001$); LLaMA-8B gains $+3.33$\,pp at $w{=}3$ (n.s.)\ but reaches significance at $w{=}1$ ($p = 0.001$). Stronger generators show no improvement---while same-model SICA \emph{degrades} ProofWriter ($-2.83$\,pp; Table~\ref{tab:main_results}), illustrating that corrective signal must originate outside the model's distribution (\S\ref{sec:mirage}). After BH correction ($m{=}12$), three conditions remain significant (Appendix~\ref{app:capability-gap}).
10	
11	\paragraph{Capability gap hypothesis.}
12	\looseness=-1 Four generators spanning a 46-point SC range on ProofWriter ($n{=}600$), all paired with DeBERTa-large-mnli (55.00\% standalone; Table~\ref{tab:cross_generator_nli}): Mistral-7B (SC 39.33\%, below the verifier) gains $+6.67$\,pp ($p < 0.001$) and LLaMA-8B (SC 70.00\%, above the verifier) gains $+3.33$\,pp at $w{=}3$ (significant at $w{=}1$, $p = 0.001$); generators above SC $\approx 74\%$ show no improvement. Per-label analysis confirms complementary error patterns that diminish as the generator improves (Appendix~\ref{app:multi-nli}). The monotonic relationship holds across three NLI architectures and six verifier-generator pairs (Spearman $\r

1	Self-Consistency (SC) majority voting over chain-of-thought traces saturates as sample count grows because same-model traces are correlated voters.
2	We investigate whether symbolic constraint extraction can break this plateau through Symbolic Invariant Consensus Aggregation (SICA), which extracts logical constraints from reasoning traces and selects answers via weighted partial MAX-SAT.
3	Across six open-source LLMs (7B--32B) on three logical reasoning domains with over 3,000 condition-evaluations (${\sim}$1,250 unique instances), we find no evidence of improvement (aggregate sign test: 5+/10$-$, $p = 0.30$; none of sixteen non-degenerate conditions shows a significant positive effect after FDR control), with equivalence established in four conditions, significant degradation in two, and ten remaining underpowered for effects below ${\sim}$2 percentage points.
4	We trace the failure to \emph{symbolic confirmation bias} (SCB): on incorrect problems, 83.3\% of self-extracted constraints support the wrong majority answer (bias ratios ranging from $1.66\times$ to $4.97\times$), but a shuffle control shows this bias is structural---it reflects trace-count distributions rather than extraction-specific error ($\text{BR}_\text{obs} \approx \text{BR}_\text{null}$, $p > 0.7$). MAX-SAT produces answers identical to count-only aggregation on 94.4\% of problems, confirming that the formal verification layer adds no information beyond majority voting despite 99\% of constraints passing Z3 validation.
5	Cross-model extraction confirms SCB universality across nine models from 7B to frontier (BR $1.66$--$4.97\times$), though frontier models use cross-model rather than self-extraction.
6	Twenty-seven training-free remediation strategies spanning fifteen families all fail, confirming a persistent independence-accuracy trade-off.
7	Oracle controls confirm the aggregation mechanism is sound ($+29.90$\,pp, $+14.22$\,pp FOLIO, $+17.17$\,pp ProofWriter; $p < 0.001$); same-model self-ext
…[truncated 531 chars]

1	\section{Introduction}\label{sec:introduction}
2	
3	Test-time computation scaling through multiple reasoning samples has emerged as a practical alternative to scaling model parameters \citep{snell2025scaling, guo2025deepseekr1}.
4	The dominant aggregation strategy, Self-Consistency (SC) \citep{wang2023sc}, samples $K$ chain-of-thought traces and selects the majority answer.
5	SC is simple, model-agnostic, and effective---but it is hitting a ceiling.
6	\citet{loo2025reevaluating} demonstrate that gains from increasing $K$ plateau and even decline for strong models.
7	The gap between SC's theoretical promise (independent correct voters converge to truth) and its practical behavior (saturating gains) points to a structural mismatch: same-model traces carry correlated errors, and majority voting has no mechanism to correct shared bias.
8	
9	Confidence-weighted \citep{taubenfeld2025cisc} and universal \citep{chen2024usc} extensions alleviate this, but the core limitation persists.
10	Recent neuro-symbolic methods \citep{pan2023logiclm, olausson2023linc, xu2026dylos, li2025aristotle, wang2025fover, luo2025logicagent} report gains on logical reasoning but introduce components beyond the reasoning model.
11	A simpler question precedes these: can the \emph{same} model extract useful formal constraints from its own traces---a zero-cost upgrade requiring no additional model or data?
12	No prior work has systematically evaluated this.
13	
14	We ground our analysis in the Condorcet Jury Theorem (CJT) \citep{condorcet1785}: if $K$ independent voters each answer correctly with probability $p > 0.5$, the majority converges to truth \citep{austensmith1996}.
15	When independence fails \citep{ladha1992correlated}, additional samples carry diminishing information.
16	Measuring inter-trace agreement with Fleiss' $\kappa$ \citep{fleiss1971kappa}, we find $\kappa = 0.21$--$0.82$ across instruction-tuned conditions, yielding $K_{\text{eff}}$ as an ordinal diagnostic of vote diversity ($K
…[truncated 6796 chars]

1	\enlargethispage{14\baselineskip}
2	\section{Discussion and Conclusion}
3	\label{sec:discussion}
4	\label{sec:conclusion}
5	
6	\looseness=-1
7	The three diagnostics ($K_\text{eff}$, BR, $C_0$) extend beyond SICA to any trace-aggregation method, correctly classifying all 60 tested conditions. Decision boundaries generalize prospectively to a held-out model family (\S\ref{sec:diagnostics}; Appendix~\ref{app:extended-discussion}), enabling practitioners to predict viability from a pilot run ($K{=}12$) at zero cost.
8	
9	\looseness=-1
10	SCB applies to any method extracting structured representations---self-verification \citep{huang2024selfcorrect}, self-refinement \citep{madaan2023selfrefine}, chain-of-verification \citep{dhuliawala2023chainofverification} all face the same distributional prior. Escaping requires (i)~\emph{structural independence}---the verifier must not share architecture or training data with the generator; (ii)~\emph{capability ordering}---combo gain decreases monotonically with generator SC, and domain-adapted NLI extends gains to generators with SC ${\sim}$72\%; (iii)~\emph{task-verifier alignment} (\S\ref{sec:cross-arch}). For test-time scaling \citep{chen2025sets, lifshitz2025mav}, with $K_\text{eff} \leq 3.6$ for $K{=}12$, same-model traces scale redundancy, not information---effective scaling requires structurally independent verifiers or trained signals escaping the model's prior.
11

    "new_string": "\\paragraph{Capability gap hypothesis.}\n\\looseness=-1 Four generators spanning a 47-point SC range on ProofWriter ($n{=}600$), all paired with DeBERTa-large-mnli (55.00\\% standalone; Table~\\ref{tab:cross_generator_nli}): only Mistral-7B (SC 39.17\\%, below the verifier) gains significantly ($+7.00$\\,pp, $p < 0.001$); all three generators above the verifier (SC $\\geq 72\\%$) show no significant improvement ($\\Delta \\leq +0.83$\\,pp). Per-label analysis confirms complementary error patterns that diminish as the generator improves (Appendix~\\ref{app:multi-nli}). The monotonic relationship holds across three NLI architectures and six verifier-generator pairs (Spearman $\\rho = 1.0$, $p = 0.003$, $n{=}6$; Figure~\\ref{fig:capability-gap}; Appendix~\\ref{app:capability-gap})."
  }
}
```

941-\includegraphics[width=0.78\columnwidth]{figures/capability_gap_scatter}
942-\caption{Combo gain vs.\ capability gap (verifier standalone $-$ generator SC) at $w{=}3$ across three NLI architectures. Gain decreases monotonically (Spearman $\rho = 1.0$, exact permutation $p = 0.003$, $n{=}6$; four of six conditions share the DeBERTa verifier---see Limitations); significant gains occur for generators with SC $\leq 70\%$. Stars: domain-adapted DeBERTa (99.67\% standalone; ${\sim}$values, Table~\ref{tab:finetuned-nli}).}
943:\label{fig:capability-gap}
944-\end{figure}
945-
946-Table~\ref{tab:cross_generator_nli} reports the controlled cross-generator comparison with DeBERTa-large-mnli on ProofWriter ($n{=}600$). We extend this analysis by pairing Mistral-7B with two additional NLI architectures: BART-large-mnli (46.17\% standalone, combo $+5.17$\,pp, $p = 0.0002$) and RoBERTa-large-mnli (37.67\% standalone, combo $+4.17$\,pp, $p = 0.003$). All three NLI architectures produce significant gains on Mistral-7B at $w \geq 3$.

860	
861	\paragraph{Disagreement-Routed Cascade details.}
862	\label{app:drc}
863	DRC invokes NLI only when SC consensus falls below threshold $\tau$, avoiding unnecessary verifier calls on high-agreement problems. Gains concentrate on weak generators (SC $< 40\%$), consistent with the capability-gap hypothesis (\S\ref{sec:cross-arch}). Regime-dependent bottleneck analysis appears in Appendix~\ref{app:2x2}.

723	\section{Cross-Model Kappa Analysis}
724	\label{app:cross-model-kappa}
725	
726	Table~\ref{tab:cross_model_kappa} compares trace independence across three model families on the same benchmarks. On FOLIO ($n = 204$), all four models produce $\kappa \geq 0.41$: LLaMA-3.1-8B ($\kappa = 0.41$), Mistral-7B ($\kappa = 0.44$), Qwen2.5-7B ($\kappa = 0.66$), and Qwen2.5-14B ($\kappa = 0.74$). The bottleneck is architecture-independent---no family escapes the correlation floor. Within the Qwen family, $\kappa$ increases from 0.66 (7B) to 0.74 (14B), but Mistral-7B (7B) and LLaMA-3.1-8B (8B) differ by $\Delta\kappa = 0.03$ despite belonging to different families, suggesting that cross-family variation ($\Delta\kappa$ up to 0.22 at similar scale) exceeds within-family scale effects ($\Delta\kappa = 0.08$). Disentangling scale from architecture requires controlled same-family experiments across $\geq$3 scales.

{
  "command": "python3 -c \"\nimport numpy as np\n\n# All 12 raw p-values from Director's D061 data\nconditions = [\n    ('Mistral w=1', 0.189),\n    ('Mistral w=3', 5e-6),\n    ('Mistral w=5', 6.2e-7),  # Director says <1e-6, keep exact value\n    ('LLaMA w=1', 0.359),\n    ('LLaMA w=3', 0.665),\n    ('LLaMA w=5', 0.871),\n    ('Qwen2.5 w=1', 1.000),\n    ('Qwen2.5 w=3', 0.503),\n    ('Qwen2.5 w=5', 0.360),\n    ('Qwen3 w=1', 1.0),\n    ('Qwen3 w=3', 0.4531),\n    ('Qwen3 w=5', 0.2100),\n]\n\nm = len(conditions)\n# Sort by raw p\nsorted_conds = sorted(conditions, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n# BH step-up\nbh_raw = [(name, p, p * m / (i+1)) for i, (name, p) in enumerate(sorted_conds)]\n\n# Enforce monotonicity from bottom up\nbh_adj = [None] * m\nbh_adj[-1] = min(bh_raw[-1][2], 1.0)\nfor i in range(m-2, -1, -1):\n    bh_adj[i] = min(bh_raw[i][2], bh_adj[i+1])\n    bh_adj[i] = min(bh_adj[i], 1.0)\n\nprint('Rank | Condition       | p_raw       | p_BH        | sig')\nprint('-' * 70)\nfor i, ((name, p_raw, _), p_bh) in enumerate(zip(bh_raw, bh_adj)):\n    sig = '***' if p_bh < 0.001 else ('**' if p_bh < 0.01 else ('*' if p_bh < 0.05 else 'n.s.'))\n    print(f'{i+1:4d} | {name:15s} | {p_raw:.6e} | {p_bh:.6e} | {sig}')\n\"\n",
  "description": "Compute BH adjusted p-values for 12 NLI combo conditions"
}

944	\end{figure}
945	
946	Table~\ref{tab:cross_generator_nli} reports the controlled cross-generator comparison with DeBERTa-large-mnli on ProofWriter ($n{=}600$). We extend this analysis by pairing Mistral-7B with two additional NLI architectures: BART-large-mnli (46.17\% standalone, combo $+5.17$\,pp, $p = 0.0002$) and RoBERTa-large-mnli (37.67\% standalone, combo $+4.17$\,pp, $p = 0.003$). All three NLI architectures produce significant gains on Mistral-7B at $w \geq 3$.
947	
948	Across all six ProofWriter verifier-generator pairs at $w{=}3$ (three verifiers $\times$ Mistral-7B plus three negative-gap generators $\times$ DeBERTa), combo gain decreases monotonically with the capability gap (Spearman $\rho = 1.0$, exact permutation $p = 0.003$, $n = 6$; however, four of six conditions share the DeBERTa verifier, which may reduce effective degrees of freedom---see Limitations; Figure~\ref{fig:capability-gap}). Significant gains are observed for generators with SC $\leq 70\%$ but not above $74\%$. After BH correction ($m{=}12$), Mistral-7B $w{=}3$ (adj.\ $p = 6.78 \times 10^{-5}$) and $w{=}5$ (adj.\ $p = 7.41 \times 10^{-6}$), and LLaMA-8B $w{=}1$ (adj.\ $p = 4.0 \times 10^{-3}$) survive.
949	
950	\paragraph{Robustness check.} Including all nine conditions (three verifiers $\times$ three weight levels on Mistral-7B) yields Spearman $\rho = 0.836$ (exact permutation $p = 0.007$, $n = 9$), confirming that the monotonic trend is robust to the choice of weight parameter.
951	
952	\paragraph{Full NLI combo with BH correction.}
953	\label{app:nli-combo-full}

**Qwen2.5-14B × FOLIO** (line 30): Table 说 75.49% 但 D061 重算得 75.98%，三个来源值都不一致。等 Agent 追溯。

### 其他
- diagnosis.tex 文字中 "none of fifteen non-degenerate conditions shows a significant positive effect" — 确认 D061 后这句是否仍然正确（Qwen3 PW 从 +0.67 变为 -0.17，原来唯一微弱正向的也没了，所以这句更 solid 了）

_source line 13285 · 2026-05-22T20:27:07.071857+00:00_

1	\section{Experiments}
2	\label{sec:experiments}
3	
4	\subsection{Setup}
5	\label{sec:exp-setup}
6	
7	We evaluate SICA against Self-Consistency (SC) across six models, three reasoning domains, and over 3,000 condition-evaluations (${\sim}$1,250 unique instances), with diagnostic analyses of confirmation bias and process-level independence (\S\ref{sec:analysis}).
8	
9	\paragraph{Models.} Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct, Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, Qwen3-14B (non-thinking mode)~\citep{yang2024qwen25}, Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.3~\citep{jiang2023mistral}, LLaMA-3.1-8B-Instruct~\citep{dubey2024llama3}, and QwQ-32B~\citep{qwen2024qwq} (7B--32B). We additionally evaluate SCB universality via cross-model extraction on five frontier models (gpt-4o, gpt-4.1, gpt-5.5, o3, Gemini-2.5-Pro; Table~\ref{tab:frontier-scb}), where Mistral-7B extracts constraints from the target model's traces since closed-source APIs do not expose internals suitable for self-extraction.
10	
11	\paragraph{Datasets.} FOLIO~\citep{han2022folio} ($n{=}204$, with gold logical annotations), ProofWriter depth-5~\citep{tafjord2021proofwriter} ($n{=}600$), LogiQA~\citep{liu2020logiqa} ($n{=}200$). StrategyQA~\citep{geva2021strategyqa} ($n{=}200$) serves as a domain-formalizability diagnostic. GSM8K~\citep{cobbe2021gsm8k} (exploratory, $n{=}30$) and AIME 2024 ($n{=}22$) probe the degenerate regime ($C_0 < 5\%$).
12	
13	\paragraph{Parameters.} $K{=}12$ traces at $T{=}0.7$, extraction at $T_{\text{ext}}{=}0.3$, $\alpha{=}0.5$, Z3 timeout 10s. McNemar exact test ($\alpha{=}0.05$); 95\% bootstrap CIs ($10{,}000$ resamples) for $n \geq 200$. SICA incurs $4.5{\times}$ SC wall-clock; extraction accounts for 76\% of overhead.
14	
15	
16	\subsection{Main Results}
17	\label{sec:main-results}
18	
19	Table~\ref{tab:main_results} reports accuracy across all conditions sorted by SC baseline, revealing three observation-driven accuracy regimes.
20

22	\centering
23	\small

_source line 13307 · 2026-05-22T20:29:37.283359+00:00_

590	The regime-dependent pattern in Table~\ref{tab:main_results} reflects three distinct failure modes of self-extracted logical constraints.
591	
592	\paragraph{Type A: confirmation bias (SC $\geq$ $\sim$60\%).} Strong models produce consistent reasoning traces, and constraints extracted from those traces mirror the answer distribution. When most constraints support the same candidate, MAX-SAT excludes few clauses ($|\mathcal{C}^{\setminus}| \approx 0$), the penalty term in Eq.~\ref{eq:score} vanishes, and the scoring function degenerates to weighted vote counting (Eq.~\ref{eq:degenerate}). SICA and SC then produce identical predictions. The ablation in Appendix~\ref{app:ablation} confirms this empirically: on Qwen2.5-14B (FOLIO + ProofWriter, $n = 804$), MAX-SAT and count-only baselines agree on 803 of 804 predictions.
593	
594	\paragraph{Type B: non-formalizable domains.} LogiQA problems require pragmatic and contextual reasoning that resists clean logical formalization. Qwen2.5-14B on LogiQA achieves an extraction rate of 86.4\%, but the resulting constraints have a contradiction rate of only 0.8\%: extracted formulas rarely conflict across traces, leaving MAX-SAT with no logical tension to exploit. Mistral-7B on LogiQA sits in the same SC range (51.00\%) yet produces $\Delta = +0.50$\,pp ($p = 1.0$); Qwen2.5-14B on the same domain yields $\Delta = -0.50$\,pp ($p = 1.0$). Neither model benefits, confirming that the right accuracy regime is necessary but not sufficient when the domain resists formalization.
595	
596	\paragraph{Type C: error amplification (SC $\leq$ $\sim$40\%).} When model accuracy is low, the majority of traces reach incorrect answers. Constraints extracted from wrong traces encode incorrect reasoning as formal rules. Z3 treats these rules as soft clauses with weight proportional to their frequency, so the solver preferentially satisfies the wrong constraints and penalizes traces that happen to reach the correct answer. Mistral-7B on ProofWriter

_source line 13321 · 2026-05-22T20:31:28.503989+00:00_

1	Self-Consistency (SC) majority voting over chain-of-thought traces saturates as sample count grows because same-model traces are correlated voters.
2	We investigate whether symbolic constraint extraction can break this plateau through Symbolic Invariant Consensus Aggregation (SICA), which extracts logical constraints from reasoning traces and selects answers via weighted partial MAX-SAT.
3	Across six open-source LLMs (7B--32B) on three logical reasoning domains with over 3,000 condition-evaluations (${\sim}$1,250 unique instances), we find no evidence of improvement (aggregate sign test: 5+/10$-$, $p = 0.30$; none of sixteen non-degenerate conditions shows a significant positive effect after FDR control), with equivalence established in four conditions, significant degradation in two, and ten remaining underpowered for effects below ${\sim}$2 percentage points.
4	We trace the failure to \emph{symbolic confirmation bias} (SCB): on incorrect problems, 83.3\% of self-extracted constraints support the wrong majority answer (bias ratios ranging from $1.66\times$ to $4.97\times$), but a shuffle control shows this bias is structural---it reflects trace-count distributions rather than extraction-specific error ($\text{BR}_\text{obs} \approx \text{BR}_\text{null}$, $p > 0.7$). MAX-SAT produces answers identical to count-only aggregation on 94.4\% of problems, confirming that the formal verification layer adds no information beyond majority voting despite 99\% of constraints passing Z3 validation.
5	Cross-model extraction confirms SCB universality across nine models from 7B to frontier (BR $1.66$--$4.97\times$), though frontier models use cross-model rather than self-extraction.
6	Twenty-seven training-free remediation strategies spanning fifteen families all fail, confirming a persistent independence-accuracy trade-off.
7	Oracle controls confirm the aggregation mechanism is sound ($+29.90$\,pp, $+14.22$\,pp FOLIO, $+17.17$\,pp ProofWriter; $p < 0.001$); same-model self-ext
…[truncated 549 chars]

_source line 13363 · 2026-05-22T20:47:23.546108+00:00_

1	Self-Consistency (SC) majority voting over chain-of-thought traces saturates as sample count grows because same-model traces are correlated voters.
2	We investigate whether symbolic constraint extraction can break this plateau through Symbolic Invariant Consensus Aggregation (SICA), which extracts logical constraints from reasoning traces and selects answers via weighted partial MAX-SAT.
3	Across six open-source LLMs (7B--32B) on three logical reasoning domains with over 3,000 condition-evaluations (${\sim}$1,250 unique instances), we find no evidence of improvement (aggregate sign test: 5+/10$-$, $p = 0.30$; none of sixteen non-degenerate conditions shows a significant positive effect after FDR control), with equivalence established in four conditions, significant degradation in two, and ten remaining underpowered for effects below ${\sim}$2 percentage points.
4	We trace the failure to \emph{symbolic confirmation bias} (SCB): on incorrect problems, 83.3\% of self-extracted constraints support the wrong majority answer (bias ratios ranging from $1.66\times$ to $4.97\times$), but a shuffle control shows this bias is structural---it reflects trace-count distributions rather than extraction-specific error ($\text{BR}_\text{obs} \approx \text{BR}_\text{null}$, $p > 0.7$). MAX-SAT produces answers identical to count-only aggregation on 94.4\% of problems, confirming that the formal verification layer adds no information beyond majority voting despite 99\% of constraints passing Z3 validation.
5	Cross-model extraction confirms SCB universality across nine models from 7B to frontier (BR $1.66$--$4.97\times$), though frontier models use cross-model rather than self-extraction.
6	Twenty-seven training-free remediation strategies spanning fifteen families all fail, confirming a persistent independence-accuracy trade-off.
7	Oracle controls confirm the aggregation mechanism is sound ($+29.90$\,pp, $+14.22$\,pp FOLIO, $+17.17$\,pp ProofWriter; $p < 0.001$); same-model self-ext
…[truncated 549 chars]

56	
57	\looseness=-1 No non-degenerate condition shows a significant SICA effect after FDR control (BH, $m{=}16$). Two show nominally significant degradation: Mistral-7B on ProofWriter ($-2.83$\,pp, raw $p = 0.014$) and Qwen2.5-14B on ProofWriter ($-2.50$\,pp, raw $p = 0.011$); adjusted $p = 0.112$ for both. The intermediate-SC region (50--60\%) contains the only positive trends, none reaching $p < 0.05$. An aggregate sign test across self-extraction conditions yields 5 positive and 10 negative $\Delta$ values ($p = 0.30$, two-sided); including all conditions gives 8+/15$-$ ($p = 0.21$). Three-seed replication on Mistral-7B confirms seed-level noise: two of three seeds produce null or negative $\Delta$ (mean $+0.16 \pm 2.70$\,pp; Table~\ref{tab:multi-seed}). Four-seed replication on Qwen2.5-14B $\times$ FOLIO confirms the single-seed pattern: $\Delta \in [0.00, -2.01]$\,pp (mean $-1.24 \pm 0.86$; Table~\ref{tab:multi-seed}). TOST equivalence tests \citep{lakens2017equivalence} with margin $\pm 2$\,pp confirm 4 equivalent, 2 genuinely different (both negative), 10 underpowered. For the 10 underpowered conditions, our tests cannot rule out effects smaller than 1.86--4.84\,pp at 80\% power; the largest study ($n{=}804$) achieves MDE $= 1.86$\,pp (Appendix~\ref{app:power}). Absence of evidence is not evidence of absence for small effects. Margin sensitivity is robust: at $\pm 1$\,pp, 0/16 equivalent; at $\pm 3$\,pp, 10/16 equivalent; no margin yields a significant positive effect (Appendix Table~\ref{tab:tost-sensitivity}). We adopt $\pm 2$\,pp based on the lower bound of SC literature gains (2--5\,pp \citep{wang2023sc}), acknowledging this choice is anchored to SC effect sizes. We trace the failure below.

_source line 13399 · 2026-05-23T00:45:52.010192+00:00_

[Director 指令] **战略写作重构任务 — 目标 4.0+**

当前状态：Round 14 评分 3.5（Borderline Conference），审稿人明确指出升分路径是 presentation 改进而非新实验。距 DDL 5/25 还有 2 天。

## 核心问题

论文实验覆盖度和统计严谨性已达顶会水准（Soundness 4/5），但 Significance（2.5-3.5/5）和 Presentation 拖了后腿。需要从整体上重新思考写作策略，而非局部修补。

## 审稿人识别的关键问题

1. **信息密度过高**：8 页正文 13 个技术缩写，审稿人认知负担大
2. **深浅不均**：§3.1 CJT 推导（非原创）占半页，§5.5 Remediation（27 种修复）只有 ~15 行，§7 Discussion 只有 1/3 页
3. **Pages 3-5 纯文字墙**：Figure 1 在 p2，Table 1 在 p6，中间 3 页无 display item
4. **Symbolic Mirage Hypothesis 措辞**：Predictions 1-2 是事后总结，只有 Prediction 3 是真正的 prediction。需要重定位为 observations + testable prediction
5. **Cross-architecture escape 结论过强**：只有 Mistral-7B 显著，需软化为 preliminary evidence
6. **Abstract power caveat 不足**：10/16 条件 underpowered
7. **惊喜度不够**：核心发现在 Huang et al. 之后可预见性高。需要突出 Z3 formal solver 场景的特殊性 — "即使加了外部求解器，extraction 偏差仍使验证层失效"

## 你的任务

**从整体反思**论文的写作策略，给出一个完整的重构方案。不要局部修补，要考虑：

1. **8 页空间的最优分配**：什么该留在正文？什么该移到附录？哪些内容值得深入展开？
2. **叙事重心**：当前论文试图在 8 页展示所有东西，结果每个都"点到即止"。选 3-4 个最强贡献深入展开，其余精简或移附录。
3. **惊喜度提升**：如何让 framing 更有冲击力？"Z3 formal solver 也被 extraction bias 击败"比"又一个 self-correction 失败"更有新闻价值。
4. **可视化策略**：Pages 3-5 的文字墙如何打破？是否需要新的 figure/table？
5. **术语瘦身**：13 个缩写哪些是必须的？哪些可以用自然语言替代？
6. **Mirage Hypothesis 重构**：怎么把 observations 和 predictions 分开，让框架更有预测力？
7. **Discussion 扩展**：三个零成本诊断 60/60 分类是很强的实用贡献，如何充分展开让审稿人 get 到价值？

**如果你的方案需要补充实验或分析**（比如新的 figure 数据、新的统计量、新的 baseline 对比），直接在方案中列出，我会让 Agent 全力支持。

请先输出 writing_plan.yaml 提交给我审批，再执行。投稿目标：EMNLP 2026 ARR May 25。引用需要验证。

1	\section{Introduction}\label{sec:introduction}
2	
3	Test-time computation scaling through multiple reasoning samples has emerged as a practical alternative to scaling model parameters \citep{snell2025scaling, guo2025deepseekr1}.
4	The dominant aggregation strategy, Self-Consistency (SC) \citep{wang2023sc}, samples $K$ chain-of-thought traces and selects the majority answer.
5	SC is simple, model-agnostic, and effective---but it is hitting a ceiling.
6	\citet{loo2025reevaluating} demonstrate that gains from increasing $K$ plateau and even decline for strong models.
7	The gap between SC's theoretical promise (independent correct voters converge to truth) and its practical behavior (saturating gains) points to a structural mismatch: same-model traces carry correlated errors, and majority voting has no mechanism to correct shared bias.
8	
9	Confidence-weighted \citep{taubenfeld2025cisc} and universal \citep{chen2024usc} extensions alleviate this, but the core limitation persists.
10	Recent neuro-symbolic methods \citep{pan2023logiclm, olausson2023linc, xu2026dylos, li2025aristotle, wang2025fover, luo2025logicagent} report gains on logical reasoning but introduce components beyond the reasoning model.
11	A simpler question precedes these: can the \emph{same} model extract useful formal constraints from its own traces---a zero-cost upgrade requiring no additional model or data?
12	No prior work has systematically evaluated this.
13	
14	We ground our analysis in the Condorcet Jury Theorem (CJT) \citep{condorcet1785}: if $K$ independent voters each answer correctly with probability $p > 0.5$, the majority converges to truth \citep{austensmith1996}.
15	When independence fails \citep{ladha1992correlated}, additional samples carry diminishing information.
16	Measuring inter-trace agreement with Fleiss' $\kappa$ \citep{fleiss1971kappa}, we find $\kappa = 0.21$--$0.82$ across instruction-tuned conditions, yielding $K_{\text{eff}}$ as an ordinal diagnostic of vote diversity ($K
…[truncated 6808 chars]

1	\section{Background \& Setup}
2	\label{sec:method}
3	
4	\subsection{Self-Consistency and the Condorcet Independence Assumption}
5	\label{sec:condorcet}
6	
7	Self-Consistency (SC) \citep{wang2023sc} samples $K$ reasoning traces and selects the majority answer. The Condorcet Jury Theorem (CJT) \citep{condorcet1785} guarantees majority convergence under independent voters \citep{austensmith1996}, but LLM traces violate this independence. Following \citet{ladha1992correlated} and \citet{kish1965survey}, we define effective sample size using Fleiss' $\kappa$ \citep{fleiss1971kappa} as proxy for intraclass correlation:
8	\begin{equation}
9	\label{eq:effk}
10	K_{\text{eff}} = K \,/\, [1 + (K{-}1) \cdot \kappa].
11	\end{equation} In our experiments (\S\ref{sec:effective-k}), $\kappa = 0.21$--$0.82$ yields $K_{\text{eff}} \approx 1.2$--$3.6$ for $K{=}12$, serving as an ordinal diagnostic of vote diversity rather than a literal effective sample size. This independence limitation motivates our investigation.
12	
13	\subsection{SICA Pipeline}
14	\label{sec:pipeline}
15	
16	Symbolic Invariant Consensus Aggregation (SICA) extends SC by extracting formal constraints from traces and selecting the answer best supported by the satisfiable subset (Figure~\ref{fig:pipeline}).
17	
18	\paragraph{Trace generation.} Given question $q$, an LLM generates $K$ traces $\{t_1, \ldots, t_K\}$ at temperature $T = 0.7$. Each trace $t_i$ yields answer $a_i$.
19	
20	\paragraph{Constraint extraction.}\label{sec:constraint-extraction} The same LLM extracts logical constraints from each trace at $T_{\text{ext}} = 0.3$. The extraction prompt requests JSON output with constraint type, expression, and Z3-compatible formula (Appendix~\ref{app:prompts}); in practice, 99.0\% of extracted constraints are propositional at the Z3 level, with only 1.0\% using true quantifiers. The \emph{extraction rate} is the fraction of traces yielding at least one parseable constraint.
21

1	\section{Cross-Architecture Proof-of-Concept}
2	\label{sec:solutions}
3	\label{sec:cross-arch}
4	
5	\paragraph{Method.}
6	\looseness=-1 We test with DeBERTa-large \citep{he2021deberta} (406M, encoder-only), fine-tuned on MultiNLI \citep{williams2018mnli}, classifying (premise, conclusion) pairs as entailment, contradiction, or neutral. In combo mode, each NLI-supporting vote counts $w$ times alongside SC's $K$ votes. We evaluate on Mistral-7B FOLIO ($n = 204$) and ProofWriter depth-5 ($n = 600$).
7	
8	\paragraph{Main result.}
9	\looseness=-1 DeBERTa NLI alone achieves 49.51\% on FOLIO (below SC's 55.88\%) and 55.00\% on ProofWriter (above SC's 39.33\%). On FOLIO ($n = 204$), combo $w{=}3$ yields $+2.94$\,pp (n.s.). On ProofWriter ($n{=}600$), combo $w{=}3$ yields a significant gain only for Mistral-7B ($+6.67$\,pp, $p < 0.001$); all generators with SC $\geq 72\%$ show no significant improvement ($\Delta \leq +0.83$\,pp, $p > 0.4$). Same-model SICA \emph{degrades} ProofWriter ($-2.83$\,pp; Table~\ref{tab:main_results}), illustrating that corrective signal must originate outside the model's distribution (\S\ref{sec:mirage}). After BH correction ($m{=}12$), two conditions remain significant (Appendix~\ref{app:capability-gap}).
10	
11	\paragraph{Capability gap hypothesis.}
12	\looseness=-1 Four generators spanning a 47-point SC range on ProofWriter ($n{=}600$), all paired with DeBERTa-large-mnli (55.00\% standalone; Table~\ref{tab:cross_generator_nli}): only Mistral-7B (SC 39.33\%, below the verifier) gains significantly ($+6.67$\,pp, $p < 0.001$); all three generators above the verifier (SC $\geq 72\%$) show no significant improvement ($\Delta \leq +0.83$\,pp). Per-label analysis confirms complementary error patterns that diminish as the generator improves (Appendix~\ref{app:multi-nli}). 1	\enlargethispage{14\baselineskip}
2	\section{Discussion and Conclusion}
3	\label{sec:discussion}
4	\label{sec:conclusion}
5	
6	\looseness=-1
7	The three diagnostics ($K_\text{eff}$, BR, $C_0$) extend beyond SICA to any trace-aggregation method, correctly classifying all 60 tested conditions. Decision boundaries generalize prospectively to a held-out model family (\S\ref{sec:diagnostics}; Appendix~\ref{app:extended-discussion}), enabling practitioners to predict viability from a pilot run ($K{=}12$) at zero cost.
8	
9	\looseness=-1
10	SCB applies to any method extracting structured representations---self-verification \citep{huang2024selfcorrect}, self-refinement \citep{madaan2023selfrefine}, chain-of-verification \citep{dhuliawala2023chainofverification} all face the same distributional prior. Escaping requires (i)~\emph{structural independence}---the verifier must not share architecture or training data with the generator; (ii)~\emph{capability ordering}---combo gain decreases monotonically with generator SC; zero-shot NLI benefits only the weakest generator, while domain-adapted NLI extends gains to generators with SC up to 72\%; (iii)~\emph{task-verifier alignment} (\S\ref{sec:cross-arch}). For test-time scaling \citep{chen2025sets, lifshitz2025mav}, with $K_\text{eff} \leq 3.6$ for $K{=}12$, same-model traces scale redundancy, not information---effective scaling requires structurally independent verifiers or trained signals escaping the model's prior.
11

1	\section{Related Work}
2	\label{sec:related-work}
3	
4	\paragraph{Self-Consistency and extensions.}
5	SC selects the most frequent answer across chain-of-thought traces \citep{wang2023sc}. Extensions include universal SC for free-form generation \citep{chen2024usc}, confidence weighting \citep{taubenfeld2025cisc}, diverse prompting with step-aware verification \citep{li2023diverse}, program-based vote filtering \citep{toh2024notallvotes}, and reasoning-aware filtering \citep{wan2024rasc, kim2026reasc}. \citet{loo2025reevaluating} show that SC gains plateau early; \citet{byerly2026selfconsistency} further analyze SC's limitations in mathematical reasoning. Our Condorcet analysis explains this: $\kappa = 0.21$--$0.82$ compresses $K_{\text{eff}}$ to 1.20--3.62 for $K = 12$ (\S\ref{sec:effective-k}).
6	
7	\paragraph{Neuro-symbolic reasoning.}
8	We distinguish three paradigms. \emph{Specification-generation} (Logic-LM \citep{pan2023logiclm}, LINC \citep{olausson2023linc}, SatLM \citep{ye2024satlm}) translates problem descriptions into formal specifications before any answer is generated; LINC documents ${\sim}$40\% translation errors even at this level. \emph{Program-generation} \citep{gao2023pal,chen2023program,lyu2023faithful} produces executable code from problem statements. Both paradigms formalize \emph{before} answer generation. \emph{Trace-extraction} (this work) operates post-hoc on traces that already contain candidates, inheriting confirmation bias (\S\ref{sec:confirmation-bias}). Recent extensions \citep{ryu2025clover, xu2026dylos, li2025aristotle, wang2025fover, singh2026verge, luo2025logicagent, miya2025eidoku} introduce capabilities beyond the reasoning model. \citet{amonkar2025realitycheck} show formalization degrades with complexity; \citet{chan2026position} argue logical soundness is unreliable for neurosymbolic verification. Our oracle controls confirm the mechanism is sound ($+29.90$/$+13.73$\,pp FOLIO, $+17.17$\,pp ProofWriter); the failure localize
…[truncated 3862 chars]

1	\section{Analysis}
2	\label{sec:analysis}
3	
4	\looseness=-1 We test the three predictions of the symbolic mirage hypothesis (\S\ref{sec:mirage}).
5	
6	\subsection{Condorcet Effective-K}
7	\label{sec:effective-k}
8	
9	\looseness=-1 Table~\ref{tab:independence} (Appendix) reports Fleiss' $\kappa$ and Effective-K (Eq.~\ref{eq:effk}) across 15 conditions at $K = 12$. Among instruction-tuned models, $\kappa$ ranges from 0.21 to 0.82, yielding $K_{\text{eff}} = 1.20\text{--}3.62$ as an ordinal diagnostic: higher $K_{\text{eff}}$ indicates greater vote diversity, though it should not be interpreted as a literal effective sample size (\S\ref{sec:condorcet}). Across four Mistral-7B seeds, $\kappa \in [0.41, 0.51]$ yet $\Delta$ ranges from $+3.43$ to $-0.49$\,pp, indicating seed-level constraint quality matters beyond independence. Positive $\Delta$ requires both sufficient $K_{\text{eff}}$ ($\leq 1.30$ never improves) and SC accuracy ($K_{\text{eff}} = 3.62$ with SC $= 39\%$ degrades). All four model families produce $\kappa \geq 0.41$ (Table~\ref{tab:kappa_generalization}).
10	
11	
12	\subsection{Symbolic Confirmation Bias}
13	\label{sec:confirmation-bias}
14	
15	\looseness=-1 Self-extracted constraints systematically align with the model's majority answer. On Mistral-7B FOLIO, when SICA errs, 83.3\% of constraints support the incorrect answer---bias ratio $4.97\times$ (Table~\ref{tab:cross-model-bias})---and 94\% of errors overlap with SC. A shuffle control reveals this bias is \emph{structural}: randomizing constraint--answer associations while preserving per-trace counts yields $\text{BR}_\text{null} \approx \text{BR}_\text{obs}$ ($p > 0.7$), indicating that BR reflects the trace-count distribution rather than extraction-specific error. Z3 rejects only 3.4\% of constraints, and MAX-SAT produces answers identical to count-only aggregation on 94.4\% of problems on Mistral-7B FOLIO (Table~\ref{tab:ablation}), confirming that the formal verification layer is vacuous (Eq.~

1	\looseness=-1 Open-source models range from 7B to 32B parameters with self-extraction; frontier models confirm SCB universality but use cross-model extraction via Mistral-7B, as closed-source APIs do not expose internals---whether self-extraction on frontier models would yield different BR values remains untested. All datasets are English-language logical reasoning benchmarks; we do not evaluate trained symbolic extractors, multilingual settings, or commonsense domains lacking gold annotations. NLI verification was tested only on propositional/first-order logic tasks; domain-adapted NLI amplifies gains dramatically but ProofWriter's synthetic templates contribute to the high verifier accuracy; generalization to naturalistic benchmarks and non-logical reasoning domains remains untested. Syntactic deduplication (46.0\% removal rate) may retain semantically equivalent constraints; however, the shuffle control (\S\ref{sec:confirmation-bias}) preserves the same duplication pattern and yields $\text{BR}_\text{shuffle} \approx \text{BR}_\text{obs}$, suggesting deduplication granularity does not drive the observed bias direction. McNemar power is below 21\% for $|\Delta| = 2$\,pp at $n{=}200$--$600$, meaning small true effects may go undetected; most conditions use a single seed; multi-seed replication on Mistral-7B (3 seeds) and Qwen2.5-14B (4 seeds) FOLIO confirms the single-seed patterns ($\Delta$ range spans ${\sim}6$\,pp and ${\sim}2$\,pp, respectively; Table~\ref{tab:multi-seed}), with multi-seed data for remaining conditions in progress. The capability-gap analysis rests on $n{=}6$ conditions sharing verifier components ($p = 0.167$ after collapsing); we characterize it as a consistent pattern rather than a confirmed law. SICA requires $4.5\times$ the wall-clock time of SC with no significant accuracy gain. Extended limitations are discussed in Appendix~\ref{app:limitations-extended}.

11	\section{Prompt Templates}
12	\label{app:prompts}
13	
14	SICA uses domain-specific prompt templates for constraint extraction: one for mathematical reasoning (GSM8K, AIME) and one for logical reasoning (ProofWriter, FOLIO). Both share the same system prompt. The LLM is called with temperature $T{=}0.3$, max tokens $1024$, and structured JSON output mode. Up to 6 traces are processed in parallel via thread pooling, with 2 retries on failure (exponential backoff, base 0.5s).
15	
16	\paragraph{System Prompt.}
17	
18	\begin{lstlisting}
19	You are a constraint extraction tool. Output ONLY
20	valid JSON. Do NOT solve problems, reason about
21	correctness, or think step-by-step. Extract
22	constraints from the given trace exactly as stated.
23	\end{lstlisting}
24	
25	\paragraph{Mathematical Constraint Extraction (EXTRACTION\_PROMPT).}
26	
27	Used for GSM8K and AIME datasets. The placeholder \texttt{\{trace\}} is replaced with the reasoning trace at runtime.
28	
29	\begin{lstlisting}
30	Extract mathematical constraints from this reasoning
31	trace as a JSON object.
32	
33	Rules:
34	- type: "equation", "inequality", or "predicate"
35	- expression: human-readable, e.g. "x + y == 14"
36	- z3_formula: a single comparison expression in
37	  Python Z3 syntax
38	- source_step: step number where the constraint
39	  appears
40	- Use variable names from the trace
41	
42	z3_formula MUST follow these rules:
43	- Allowed operators: ==, !=, <, >, <=, >=, +, -, *,
44	  /, **, %
45	- Use individual named variables

14	  - agent
15	  - worker
16	  - paper_agent
17	---
18	
19	# 论文写作风格规范
20	
21	> 核心哲学：**论文是一个有说服力的故事，不是实验报告。** 审稿人在几十秒内判断论文价值——如果 Abstract 和 Introduction 读起来像实验日志，论文就已经失败了。
22	
23	---
24	
25	## 一、总体写作哲学
26	
27	### 1.1 故事驱动，不是数据堆砌
28	
29	- 论文必须以"故事线"为核心展开：问题 → 洞察 → 方法 → 验证
30	- 禁止大量实验数据堆砌——每个数字出现必须服务于叙事推进
31	- 附录内容必须在正文中有明确引用且融入叙事，禁止简单堆砌
32	- 如果附录数据对核心叙事有重大影响，根据附录数据调整论文 framing
33	
34	### 1.2 主线统一性
35	
36	- 当 idea 在研究过程中发生变化后，**必须重审全文逻辑**
37	- 常见问题：Introduction 讲的故事 A，Method 做的是故事 B，Results 又在讲故事 C
38	- 修复方式：从最终实验结论反推，重写 Introduction 的问题定义和动机
39	- 每次 idea 调整后检查：Abstract → Introduction → Method → Results → Discussion → Conclusion 是否形成一条连贯主线
40	
41	### 1.3 专有名词必须有定义
42	
43	- 首次出现的专有名词/缩写必须给出完整定义和解释
44	- Abstract 中使用的缩写也必须在 Abstract 内定义（如 LLM = Large Language Model）
45	- 不能假设读者知道你自己定义的术语——论文从来没解释过的概念不能直接使用
46	- 通用规则：如果一个非标准术语第一次出现时没有括号定义，就是写作 bug
47	
48	### 1.4 写作前必读同类优秀论文
49	
50	- 每个章节动笔前，找 **2-3 篇目标会议近 1-2 年的高分论文**作为风格参考——同类问题或同类方法皆可
51	- 重点学：
52	  - Abstract 怎么用最少篇幅讲完故事
53	  - Introduction 怎么从大问题切入到具体贡献
54	  - Related Work 怎么 positioning 自己（不是 survey）
55	  - Table/Figure 怎么压缩信息密度
56	- **不是抄袭**，是学习同行的故事节奏和审美——不同子领域叙事节奏差异很大
57	- 实操：Paper Agent 可以从 `literature_map.yaml` 中挑 `role=foundation` 或 `role=direct-competitor` 的论文，把 `{title, venue, year, abstract}` 附在 Worker 的 task_prompt 中作为概念参考；如需学习写作风格本身，让 Worker 通过 WebSearch 取得论文链接并 fetch 全文
58	
59	---
60	
61	## 二、Abstract 写作规范
62	
63	### 2.1 四个必答问题（150-250 words）
64	
65	Abstract 必须在很短的篇幅内清楚回答：
66	1. **研究什么问题** — 1-2 句话交代背景和痛点
67	2. **为什么重要** — 现有方法的不足（不用写太多）
68	3. **本文提出什么** — 核心贡献的关键机制（简要，不堆砌细节）
69	4. **结果说明什么** — 用具体实验结论支撑贡献
70	
71	### 2.2 禁忌
72	
73	- **禁止堆砌数据**：Abstract 不是 Results 的缩写。最多引用 2-3 个关键数字，要服务于 claim
74	- **
…[truncated 194 chars]

4	allowed-tools: [Read, Write, Edit, Glob, Grep]

6	version: 1
7	tags:
8	  - writing

18	---
19	
20	# 论文写作质量指南（NeurIPS 实战经验）
21	
22	> 来源：2026 年 NeurIPS 投稿期间，从 20+ 项目的人工审查反馈中提炼。
23	> 每条规则都源自真实的 bug / 退回 / 审稿质疑。
24	
25	---
26	
27	## 一、数据诚信与溯源（最高优先级）
28	
29	### 1.1 数字必须有源头
30	
31	- 论文中**每一个数字**必须可追溯到 registry 实验 ID 或原始结果文件
32	- 禁止凭记忆写数字——必须 `cat result.json | head` + `ls -la` 附上文件证据
33	- 计算值（均值、标准差、DID、delta）必须独立重算，不信 Agent 第一手汇报
34	- `paper_provenance.yaml` 覆盖率必须 100%，改数字必须同步更新 provenance
35	
36	> **教训（D149 事件）**：Agent 曾伪造完整的 metrics 数据包括 p-value，事后被 Reviewer 独立 parse JSON 抓出。从此所有关键数据必须 Reviewer 独立验证到字段级别。
37	
38	### 1.2 不同来源数据不能混用
39	
40	- 配对数据必须同 seed、同 c 值、同 N、同 evaluation protocol
41	- 两张表如果 protocol 不同（如 0-shot vs 8-shot），论文必须明确说明
42	- 跨 config 比较 delta 会引入系统误差——必须补跑同 config baseline 后才能比较
43	- 非等量对比必须显式标注（如 "at unmatched volumes"）
44	
45	> **教训（D027 事件）**：不同 config 文件的 baseline 不同，跨 config 比较 delta 值引入了 artifact。
46	
47	### 1.3 结论撤回后不可复活
48	
49	- 被 Director shelved 的数据（如因截断混淆），禁止重新引入论文
50	- `paper_provenance.yaml` 中 `verified: false` 的 claim，不得恢复
51	- 术语修正后不可回退（如 "same norm" → "same per-layer standard deviation"）
52	
53	---
54	
55	## 二、Claim 强度与数据匹配
56	
57	### 2.1 Claim 强度规则
58	
59	| 数据支撑 | 允许的最强表述 |
60	|----------|--------------|
61	| 单 seed, 单数据集 | "preliminary observation", "initial evidence" |
62	| 单 seed, 多数据集 | "suggestive pattern", "indicative trend" |
63	| 多 seed, 单数据集 | 报告均值±标准差，可写 "we find" |
64	| 多 seed, 多数据集 | 可用 "robust", "consistent" |
65	| 统计检验显著 | 可用 "significant", 附 p-value |
66	| 单 seed + 外推到其他条件 | 必须加限定，不能声称 "scale-invariant" 等 |
67	
68	### 2.2 禁止的过强表述
69	
70	- "remarkably stable" → 改为 "relatively stable" 或给出量化波动范围
71	- "confirmed" → 仅 "suggestive" 级别数据不能用
72	- "
…[truncated 366 chars]

  "content": "# Writing Plan — symb_invariant_consensus\n# V4 STRATEGIC RESTRUCTURE: Presentation-first upgrade for 4.0+\n# Target: EMNLP 2026 ARR May cycle, DDL 2026-05-25\n# Updated: 2026-05-23T16:00:00Z\n\nmeta:\n  venue: EMNLP 2026\n  track: main (ARR May cycle)\n  template: docs/paper/acl.sty\n  page_limit: 8\n  deadline: 2026-05-25\n  title: >\n    Beyond Self-Consistency: Why Self-Extracted Constraints\n    Fail to Improve LLM Logical Reasoning\n  title_alternative: >\n    The Symbolic Mirage: Why Formal Self-Verification\n    Adds No Information Beyond Majority Voting\n  title_note: >\n    Alternative title is punchier and highlights the paradox.\n    Director decides. Current title is safe; alternative is higher-impact.\n  core_claim: >\n    Provably sound Z3 verification becomes informationally vacuous\n    under self-extraction: MAX-SAT agrees with majority voting on\n    94.4% of problems because self-extracted constraints inherit\n    the model's distributional bias (BR 1.66-4.97x across nine models).\n    Three zero-cost diagnostics classify all 60 tested conditions.\n    Cross-architecture NLI provides preliminary evidence of escaping\n    the bottleneck.\n  paper_type: >\n    Diagnostic negative-result paper with constructive escape.\n    Primary value: quantified diagnosis + practitioner framework.\n  previous_version: \"v3 (2026-05-20): G+R Dual Method (abandoned)\"\n  change_rationale: >\n    Round 14 scored 3.5. Soundness is strong (4/5) but Significance\n    (2.5-3.5) and Presentation drag. Reviewer pathway to 4.0+ is\n    presentation improvement, not new experiments. V4 restructures\n    for: (1) narrative clarity, (2) display-item redistribution,\n    (3) abbreviation reduction, (4) depth rebalancing.\n\n# ──────────────────────────────────────────────\n# DIAGNOSIS: 7 Problems → 7 Solutions\n# ──────────────────────────────────────────────\nrestructure_rationale:\n\n  problem_1_info_density:\n    issue: \"13 technical abbreviations in 8 pages\"\n    solution: \"Reduce to 6 essential abbreviations\"\n    keep: [SC, SICA, BR, \"K_eff\", NLI, MAX-SAT]\n    eliminate:\n      - SCB: \"spell out 'symbolic confirmation bias' (use abbreviation only in tables)\"\n      - CJT: \"spell out 'Condorcet Jury Theorem' (only used 2-3 times)\"\n      - CCR: \"appendix only\"\n      - DRC: \"appendix only\"\n      - ICG: \"spell out 'iterative constraint generation'\"\n      - ProcessSim: \"use 'process similarity'\"\n      - TOST: \"spell out or remove from main text\"\n      - FOL: \"use 'logical constraints' or spell out\"\n    net_reduction: \"13 → 6 abbreviations (-54%)\"\n\n  problem_2_depth_imbalance:\n    issue: \"§3.1 CJT (half page, non-original) vs §5.4 Remediation (15 lines, major finding) vs §7 Discussion (1/3 page)\"\n    solution: |\n      - CJT: 2 sentences in intro (save 0.4 pages)\n      - Remediation: expand to 25-30 lines (independence-accuracy trade-off is key)\n      - Discussion: expand from 2 to 4 paragraphs (diagnostics 60/60 is strongest practical contribution)\n      - Mirage Hypothesis: remove from §3, present in §5 emerging from data\n\n  problem_3_text_wall:\n    issue: \"Pages 3-5 have zero display items (Related Work + Background & Setup)\"\n    solution: |\n      - Move experimental setup into §3 Method → Table 1 appears ~page 4-5 (was page 6)\n      - Move regime scatter figure into §4 Results → visual relief on page 5\n      - Add diagnostic decision tree figure in §5 → another display item\n      - Net: every 2-page spread gets at least one display item\n\n  problem_4_mirage_hypothesis:\n    issue: \"Empirical Finding 1-2 are post-hoc observations in theorem-like boxes; only P1 is testable\"\n    solution: |\n      - Remove Finding 1 and Finding 2 tcolorboxes from §3\n      - Remove O1/O2/P1 labels (too formulaic for observations)\n      - Present the Mirage Hypothesis in §5 AFTER showing SCB data\n      - Frame as: \"Three patterns emerge from the data → we formulate the Symbolic Mirage\"\n      - The hypothesis is then TESTED by §6 (cross-architecture)\n      - More natural scientific narrative: data → hypothesis → test\n\n  problem_5_cross_arch_overstatement:\n    issue: \"Cross-architecture results treated as established finding; only Mistral-7B is significant\"\n    solution: |\n      - \"escapes the bottleneck\" → \"preliminary evidence of escaping\"\n      - \"validates\" → \"is consistent with\"\n      - Add explicit caveat: \"significant only for Mistral-7B (SC below verifier)\"\n      - In abstract: add \"on the weakest generator\" qualifier\n      - Section title: \"Escaping the Bottleneck\" → keep (describes aspiration)\n\n  problem_6_power_caveat:\n    issue: \"10/16 conditions underpowered; abstract doesn't acknowledge\"\n    solution: |\n      - Abstract: add \"with equivalence established in four conditions,\n        ten remaining underpowered for effects below ~2 percentage points\"\n      - Results: keep existing TOST discussion but condense to essentials\n\n  problem_7_surprise:\n    issue: \"Core finding predictable after Huang et al.; Z3 specificity underemphasized\"\n    solution: |\n      Reframe the hook throughout the paper:\n      - OLD: \"Another self-correction failure study\"\n      - NEW: \"Even provably sound formal verification becomes vacuous under self-extraction\"\n      Key differentiators to emphasize:\n      1. Z3 is a FORMAL solver (not another LLM trick) — provably correct\n      2. 99% of constraints pass Z3 validation — no syntax issues\n      3. Oracle controls prove the MECHANISM works (+30pp possible)\n      4. The failure is specifically in EXTRACTION — new kind of bias\n      5. MAX-SAT = majority voting on 94.4% of problems — the formal apparatus is a \"mirage\"\n      This is distinct from Huang: they show NL self-correction fails;\n      we show FORMAL self-verification fails even with a correct solver.\n\n# ──────────────────────────────────────────────\n# NEW SECTION STRUCTURE\n# ──────────────────────────────────────────────\npaper_outline:\n\n  - section_id: abstract\n    file: abstract.tex\n    title: Abstract\n    word_budget: 220\n    change_type: rewrite\n    key_changes:\n      - \"Lead with paradox: formal verification + self-extraction = vacuous\"\n      - \"Add power caveat: ten conditions underpowered\"\n      - \"Soften cross-architecture: 'preliminary evidence', 'on the weakest generator'\"\n      - \"Reduce number density: max 3-4 key numbers\"\n      - \"Eliminate SCB abbreviation (spell out)\"\n\n  - section_id: sec1_introduction\n    file: introduction.tex\n    title: Introduction\n    page_budget: 1.0\n    word_budget: 800\n    change_type: rewrite\n    key_changes:\n      - \"Sharper hook: Z3 formal solver paradox as the opening move\"\n      - \"CJT as 2 sentences (not a subsection reference) — just Keff equation mention\"\n      - \"Contribution list: 3 items, max 3 lines each (currently 5-8 lines each)\"\n      - \"Add power caveat to results preview\"\n      - \"Soften cross-architecture claim\"\n      - \"Keep Figure 1 (pipeline)\"\n    contributions_rewrite:\n      C1:\n        label: \"The Symbolic Mirage in formal self-verification\"\n        target_lines: 3\n        content: >\n          Provably sound Z3 verification becomes vacuous under self-extraction:\n          83.3% of constraints support the wrong answer (BR 1.66-4.97x, nine models),\n          MAX-SAT agrees with majority voting on 94.4% of problems. A shuffle control\n          confirms the bias is structural. 27 remediation strategies fail.\n      C2:\n        label: \"Practitioner diagnostic framework\"\n        target_lines: 2\n        content: >\n          Three zero-cost diagnostics (K_eff, BR, C0) correctly classify\n          all 60 tested conditions, providing a priori viability prediction.\n      C3:\n        label: \"Cross-architecture escape\"\n        target_lines: 2\n        content: >\n          Preliminary evidence that structurally independent NLI verification\n          escapes the bottleneck (+7pp on the weakest generator, p<0.001),\n          consistent with the diagnostic framework's predictions.\n    display_items: [fig:pipeline]\n\n  - section_id: sec2_related_work\n    file: related_work.tex\n    title: Related Work\n    page_budget: 0.65\n    word_budget: 520\n    change_type: trim\n    key_changes:\n      - \"Merge CJT paragraph into SC paragraph (save ~6 lines)\"\n      - \"Cut 'Positioning' paragraph by 50% (much repeats intro)\"\n      - \"3 tight paragraphs: SC+CJT, Neuro-symbolic, Test-time+self-verification\"\n      - \"Remove detailed method comparisons (readers can find in appendix)\"\n    display_items: []\n\n  - section_id: sec3_method\n    file: method.tex\n    title: Method\n    page_budget: 0.85\n    word_budget: 680\n    change_type: major_rewrite\n    current_subsections:\n      - \"§3.1 CJT (REMOVE — move to intro as 2 sentences)\"\n      - \"§3.2 SICA Pipeline (KEEP — equations essential)\"\n      - \"§3.3 Symbolic Mirage Hypothesis (REMOVE — move to §5)\"\n      - \"Empirical Finding 1-2 boxes (REMOVE)\"\n    new_subsections:\n      - id: sec3_pipeline\n        title: \"SICA Pipeline\"\n        content: >\n          Keep Eqs 1-4 (K_eff, MAX-SAT, scoring, degeneration).\n          Keep C0 diagnostic definition (inline, no subsection).\n          Tighten prose around equations.\n      - \"§3.1 CJT derivation → 2 sentences in intro\"\n      - \"§3.3 Mirage Hypothesis → §5 (after showing data)\"\n      - \"Empirical Finding 1 box → paragraph in §5\"\n      - \"Empirical Finding 2 box → paragraph in §6\"\n    page_savings: 0.5\n    display_items: []\n\n  - section_id: sec4_results\n    file: experiments.tex\n    title: Results\n    page_budget: 1.4\n    word_budget: 550\n    change_type: major_rewrite\n    key_changes:\n      - \"Remove §4.1 Setup (moved to §3)\"\n      - \"Keep Table 1 (tab:main_results) — now appears ~page 4-5\"\n      - \"Move regime scatter figure HERE from analysis (breaks text wall)\"\n      - \"Move regime-dependent analysis HERE from analysis §5.4\"\n      - \"Condense TOST/power to 2-3 sentences (details in appendix)\"\n      - \"Oracle mention: 1-2 sentences (details in appendix)\"\n      - \"Sign test and aggregate summary stays\"\n    display_items: [tab:main_results, fig:regime-scatter]\n    notes: >\n      Moving regime scatter + analysis here serves two purposes:\n      (1) visual relief on pages 4-5, (2) results and their\n      regime-dependent patterns belong together.\n\n  - section_id: sec5_symbolic_mirage\n    file: analysis.tex\n    title: \"The Symbolic Mirage\"\n    page_budget: 2.0\n    word_budget: 900\n    change_type: major_rewrite\n    narrative_flow: >\n      \"Why does provably sound verification fail?\" → SCB quantification\n      → shuffle control → process-level (condensed) → remediation\n      (EXPANDED) → diagnostic framework → Mirage Hypothesis formulation\n    new_subsections:\n      - id: sec5_scb\n        title: \"Symbolic Confirmation Bias\"\n        content: >\n          BR quantification, shuffle control (BR_null ≈ BR_obs),\n          Z3 vacuity (94.4% MAX-SAT=SC), constraint ablation.\n          Table: frontier-scb (cross-model universality).\n          Answer-leakage controls (2 sentences).\n      - id: sec5_process\n        title: \"Process-Level Independence\"\n        content: >\n          CONDENSE to 1 paragraph (currently a full subsection).\n          Key point: ProcessSim 0.69 >> answer-kappa 0.46.\n          Move process-kappa scatter figure to APPENDIX.\n          Keep: cross-model generalization (1 sentence).\n      - id: sec5_remediation\n        title: \"Why Remediation Fails\"\n        content: >\n          EXPAND from 15 to 25-30 lines. This is a MAJOR finding.\n          Independence-accuracy trade-off: the core insight.\n          Debate (kappa=0.15, -18.6pp) vs SC/DP/ICG (kappa 0.40-0.47).\n          Three model families all hit kappa≥0.41 ceiling.\n          27 strategies, 15 families, zero positive.\n          Figure: interventions compact (stays).\n      - id: sec5_diagnostics\n        title: \"Diagnostic Framework\"\n        content: >\n          Three diagnostics: C0, K_eff, BR with decision boundaries.\n          60/60 classification across all tested conditions.\n          Prospective validation on held-out LLaMA family.\n          NEW FIGURE: diagnostic decision tree (TikZ).\n      - id: sec5_hypothesis\n        title: null  # inline paragraph, not a subsection\n        content: >\n          Formulate the Symbolic Mirage hypothesis as emerging from\n          the data patterns above. Natural scientific narrative.\n          No tcolorboxes. No O1/O2/P1 labels. Just a clear paragraph.\n    display_items: [tab:frontier-scb, fig:interventions-summary, \"fig:diagnostic-tree (NEW)\"]\n    figure_request:\n      fig_id: diagnostic_tree\n      type: decision_tree\n      tool: tikz_inline\n      description: >\n        Diagnostic decision tree for symbolic aggregation viability.\n        Flow: K=12 pilot run → C0<5%? (Degenerate) → K_eff≤1.3?\n        (Diversity-limited) → SC≤40%? (Error amplification) → BR>2x?\n        (Confirmation bias) → External verifier? → Capability gap check\n        → Potential gain. Annotate with 60/60 classification accuracy.\n        Compact, single-column width, ~0.3 page.\n      narrative_intent: >\n        Visualize the practitioner diagnostic tool. Show that three\n        simple checks predict all outcomes. This is the paper's\n        most actionable contribution.\n      design_notes: >\n        Use TikZ directly in LaTeX (no external figure needed).\n        Rounded boxes, gray/blue color scheme matching Figure 1.\n        Decision nodes as diamonds, outcome nodes as rounded rectangles.\n        Compact vertical layout.\n\n  - section_id: sec6_escape\n    file: solutions.tex\n    title: \"Cross-Architecture Escape\"\n    page_budget: 1.0\n    word_budget: 480\n    change_type: minor_rewrite\n    key_changes:\n      - \"Soften claims: 'preliminary evidence', 'consistent with'\"\n      - \"Add explicit caveat: significant only for Mistral-7B\"\n      - \"Fix data values if D152 patch not applied (39.33→39.17, +6.67→+7.00)\"\n      - \"Frame as: 'Our diagnosis predicts what should work — is it consistent?'\"\n      - \"Keep Table 3 and capability gap scatter figure\"\n      - \"Domain-adapted: keep brief paragraph\"\n    display_items: [tab:cross_generator_nli, fig:capability-gap]\n\n  - section_id: sec7_discussion\n    file: discussion.tex\n    title: \"Discussion and Conclusion\"\n    page_budget: 0.6\n    word_budget: 480\n    change_type: major_expansion\n    current_state: \"2 short paragraphs, ~0.3 pages\"\n    new_structure:\n      para_1_diagnostics: >\n        Diagnostic framework as practitioner tool. Three checks\n        from a K=12 pilot run predict aggregation viability at zero cost.\n        60/60 conditions classified correctly. Decision boundaries\n        generalize prospectively to held-out model family.\n        Emphasize: this is actionable, not just analytical.\n      para_2_test_time: >\n        Implications for test-time scaling. With K_eff ≤ 3.6 for K=12,\n        same-model traces scale redundancy, not information.\n        Effective scaling requires structurally independent verifiers\n        or trained signals that escape the model's distributional prior.\n      para_3_self_verification: >\n        Broader implications. Symbolic confirmation bias applies to\n        any method extracting structured representations from LLM traces:\n        self-verification, self-refinement, chain-of-verification.\n        The key insight: formal soundness of the verifier is necessary\n        but not sufficient — extraction must be independent.\n      para_4_future: >\n        Future directions. Trained extractors, multi-modal verification,\n        larger-scale validation. Whether trained extractors can shift\n        the independence-accuracy trade-off boundary remains open.\n    remove: \"\\\\enlargethispage{14\\\\baselineskip} hack\"\n\n  - section_id: limitations\n    file: limitations_content.tex\n    title: Limitations\n    change_type: minor_edit\n    notes: >\n      Already concise per EMNLP rules (~3 points).\n      May need minor updates to match restructured claims.\n\n# ──────────────────────────────────────────────\n# DISPLAY ITEM REDISTRIBUTION\n# ──────────────────────────────────────────────\ndisplay_item_plan:\n  target_distribution:\n    \"pages 1-2\": \"Figure 1 (pipeline) — in introduction\"\n    \"pages 3-4\": \"Table 1 starts — moved earlier by merging setup into §3\"\n    \"pages 4-5\": \"Table 1 + Figure (regime scatter) — in §4 Results\"\n    \"pages 5-6\": \"Table (frontier-scb) — in §5 Symbolic Mirage\"\n    \"pages 6-7\": \"Figure (interventions) + Figure (diagnostic tree, NEW) — in §5\"\n    \"pages 7-8\": \"Table (cross_generator_nli) + Figure (capability gap) — in §6\"\n\n  items_in_main_text:\n    - {id: fig:pipeline, type: figure, location: \"§1 Introduction\", status: keep}\n    - {id: tab:main_results, type: table, location: \"§4 Results\", status: keep}\n    - {id: fig:regime-scatter, type: figure, location: \"§4 Results\", status: \"move from §5\"}\n    - {id: tab:frontier-scb, type: table, location: \"§5 Symbolic Mirage\", status: keep}\n    - {id: fig:interventions-summary, type: figure, location: \"§5 Symbolic Mirage\", status: keep}\n    - {id: fig:diagnostic-tree, type: figure, location: \"§5 Symbolic Mirage\", status: \"NEW (TikZ inline)\"}\n    - {id: tab:cross_generator_nli, type: table, location: \"§6 Escape\", status: keep}\n    - {id: fig:capability-gap, type: figure, location: \"§6 Escape\", status: keep}\n\n  items_moved_to_appendix:\n    - {id: fig:process-kappa, reason: \"Supporting evidence, not essential. Key stat in 1 paragraph.\"}\n    - {id: tab:independence, reason: \"Already in appendix (analysis.tex references it as 'Appendix')\"}\n    - {id: tab:kappa_generalization, reason: \"Key numbers absorbed into remediation text\"}\n\n# ──────────────────────────────────────────────\n# PAGE BUDGET\n# ──────────────────────────────────────────────\npage_budget:\n  total_limit: 8\n  target: 7.8  # leave 0.2 page buffer\n  breakdown:\n    abstract: 0.5\n    sec1_introduction: 1.0\n    sec2_related_work: 0.65\n    sec3_method: 0.85\n    sec4_results: 1.4\n    sec5_symbolic_mirage: 2.0\n    sec6_escape: 1.0\n    sec7_discussion: 0.6\n    total_estimated: 8.0\n  savings:\n    \"Remove CJT subsection from §3\": -0.4\n    \"Remove Mirage Hypothesis + Finding boxes from §3\": -0.3\n    \"Trim related work\": -0.15\n    \"Trim introduction contributions\": -0.15\n    \"Condense process-kappa to 1 paragraph\": -0.15\n  additions:\n    \"Add setup to §3\": +0.2\n    \"Add regime analysis to §4\": +0.25\n    \"Expand remediation in §5\": +0.15\n    \"Add diagnostic tree figure\": +0.2\n    \"Expand discussion\": +0.3\n  net: -0.05  # comfortable\n\n# ──────────────────────────────────────────────\n# EXECUTION PLAN (serial, 9 steps)\n# ──────────────────────────────────────────────\nexecution_order:\n  - step: 1\n    task: \"Generate diagnostic decision tree figure\"\n    task_type: paper_writing  # TikZ inline, no external figure\n    file: analysis.tex\n    description: >\n      Create TikZ decision tree for diagnostic framework.\n      Will be embedded directly in analysis.tex (no separate figure file).\n      Compile to verify layout.\n\n  - step: 2\n    task: \"Rewrite method.tex\"\n    task_type: paper_writing\n    file: method.tex\n    description: >\n      Remove CJT subsection, Mirage Hypothesis, Finding boxes.\n      Add experimental setup (from experiments.tex §4.1).\n      Keep SICA pipeline equations.     "content": "# Writing Plan — symb_invariant_consensus\n# V4 STRATEGIC RESTRUCTURE: Presentation-first upgrade for 4.0+\n# Target: EMNLP 2026 ARR May cycle, DDL 2026-05-25\n# Updated: 2026-05-23T16:00:00Z\n\nmeta:\n  venue: EMNLP 2026\n  track: main (ARR May cycle)\n  template: docs/paper/acl.sty\n  page_limit: 8\n  deadline: 2026-05-25\n  title: >\n    Beyond Self-Consistency: Why Self-Extracted Constraints\n    Fail to Improve LLM Logical Reasoning\n  title_alternative: >\n    The Symbolic Mirage: Why Formal Self-Verification\n    Adds No Information Beyond Majority Voting\n  title_note: >\n    Alternative title is punchier and highlights the paradox.\n    Director decides. Current title is safe; alternative is higher-impact.\n  core_claim: >\n    Provably sound Z3 verification becomes informationally vacuous\n    under self-extraction: MAX-SAT agrees with majority voting on\n    94.4% of problems because self-extracted constraints inherit\n    the model's distributional bias (BR 1.66-4.97x across nine models).\n    Three zero-cost diagnostics classify all 60 tested conditions.\n    Cross-architecture NLI provides preliminary evidence of escaping\n    the bottleneck.\n  paper_type: >\n    Diagnostic negative-result paper with constructive escape.\n    Primary value: quantified diagnosis + practitioner framework.\n  previous_version: \"v3 (2026-05-20): G+R Dual Method (abandoned)\"\n  change_rationale: >\n    Round 14 scored 3.5. Soundness is strong (4/5) but Significance\n    (2.5-3.5) and Presentation drag. Reviewer pathway to 4.0+ is\n    presentation improvement, not new experiments. SCB: \"spell out 'symbolic confirmation bias' (use abbreviation only in tables)\"\n      - CJT: \"spell out 'Condorcet Jury Theorem' (only used 2-3 times)\"\n      - CCR: \"appendix only\"\n      - DRC: \"appendix only\"\n      - ICG: \"spell out 'iterative constraint generation'\"\n      - ProcessSim: \"use 'process similarity'\"\n      - TOST: \"spell out or remove from main text\"\n      - FOL: \"use 'logical constraints' or spell out\"\n    net_reduction: \"13 → 6 abbreviations (-54%)\"\n\n  problem_2_depth_imbalance:\n    issue: \"§3.1 CJT (half page, non-original) vs §5.4 Remediation (15 lines, major finding) vs §7 Discussion (1/3 page)\"\n    solution: |\n      - CJT: 2 sentences in intro (save 0.4 pages)\n      - Remediation: expand to 25-30 lines (independence-accuracy trade-off is key)\n      - Discussion: expand from 2 to 4 paragraphs (diagnostics 60/60 is strongest practical contribution)\n      - Remove Finding 1 and Finding 2 tcolorboxes from §3\n      - Remove O1/O2/P1 labels (too formulaic for observations)\n      - Present the Mirage Hypothesis in §5 AFTER showing SCB data\n      - Frame as: \"Three patterns emerge from the data → we formulate the Symbolic Mirage\"\n      - _source line 13467 · 2026-05-23T00:58:05.079639+00:00_

[Director 指令] **补充指令：理论深度强化 — A/B/C/D 四个方向全做**

用户要求在写作重构的同时，加强论文的理论深度和概念强度。**质量优先于速度**。以下四个方向全部纳入你的 writing_plan：

## A. SCB 概率证明（最高优先级）

把 Symbolic Confirmation Bias 从"经验观察"升级为"理论结果"。构建简单概率模型：

- 模型 M 对问题 q 生成答案 a_M
- 从包含 a_M 的 trace 提取约束 c
- 证明 E[BR] ≥ 1：P(c supports a_M | a_M ≠ a*) > P(c supports a* | a_M ≠ a*)

核心洞察：约束 c 是 trace 的有损函数，trace 已经包含 a_M → c 继承 a_M 的分布先验。这是信息论上的必然，不是模型能力问题。

目标：在 §3.3 或 §5.2 中用 1-2 段给出 clean 的形式化论证。不需要长证明，要 elegant。

## B. CIL（Cumulative Information Lift）信息论指标

形式化定义：CIL(V, G) = I(V_output; ground_truth | G_answer)

- Self-extraction → CIL ≈ 0（约束不含超出答案的信息）
- Cross-architecture → CIL > 0（独立模型带来新信息）

这个指标如果能统一解释所有条件的 combo gain 方向，就比 K_eff + BR + C₀ 三个 ad hoc 指标更优雅。**这可能需要 Agent 用已有实验数据计算 mutual information**——如果你确定需要哪些数据，列出来，我让 Agent 准备。

## C. Capability Gap 理论模型

当前 capability gap 只有 n=6 的经验观察。需要一个简单的理论推导：

假设 verifier V（准确率 V_acc）与 generator G（准确率 SC_G）条件独立，推导 combo gain 与 SC_G 的关系。至少要能解释为什么 gain 随 SC_G 单调递减——当 SC_G → 1 时 gain → 0（天花板），当 SC_G 很低时 gain 受 V_acc 上界控制。

## D. 因果 DAG

画一张 M → T → A → C → S 的因果图，用 d-separation 论证 C ⊥ ground_truth | A。这张图直观解释了 self-extraction 失败的信息论根源：**约束的所有信息都经过"答案"这个瓶颈节点**。

可放在 §3.3（方法论基础）或作为 Figure 插入 pages 3-5 的文字墙区域（一举两得：加理论深度 + 打破纯文字）。

## 与写作重构的关系

这四个方向应该融入你的整体重构方案：
- A + D 可能改变 §3.3 Symbolic Mirage 的整个写法（从 ad hoc hypothesis → 有理论支撑的 framework）
- B 可能影响 §5 或 §6 的结构（CIL 作为统一指标替代部分 diagnostics）
- C 强化 §6 的理论基础

**质量要求**：
- 形式化论证要 rigorous 但 accessible——审稿人是 NLP 背景，不是数学家
- 每个理论结果要与已有经验数据对应（理论预测 X，实验验证 X）
- 不要为了加理论而牺牲可读性——如果推导太长，核心结果放正文，完整证明放附录

请把这四个方向整合进 writing_plan.yaml 一起提交审批。如果需要 Agent 计算新数据（比如 CIL），在 plan 中标注清楚。

200	
201	\section{Capability-Accuracy Constraint Diagnostics}
202	\label{app:diagnostics}
203	\label{app:cil}
204	
205	\paragraph{Conditional Correction Ratio.}
206	We define the \textit{Conditional Correction Ratio} (CCR) to summarize verifier--generator complementarity:
207	\begin{equation}
208	\label{eq:cil}
209	\text{CCR}(V, G) = \log \frac{P(V \text{ correct} \mid G \text{ wrong})}{P(V \text{ correct} \mid G \text{ correct})}.
210	\end{equation}
211	$\text{CCR} > 0$ means $V$ preferentially corrects $G$'s errors; $\text{CCR} < 0$ indicates shared bias. CCR links our findings: (1)~SCB corresponds to $\text{CCR}(\text{self}, G) \approx 0$; (2)~Finding~2 predicts $\text{CCR}(V, G) > 0$ when $V$ is independent and capable.
212	
213	\paragraph{Empirical CCR across verifier--generator pairs.}
214	Table~\ref{tab:cil_empirical} reports CCR for eight verifier--generator--dataset conditions, sorted by CCR descending. All three cross-architecture NLI pairs on ProofWriter yield $\text{CCR} > 0$ (verifier preferentially corrects generator errors), while all three same-model SICA conditions yield $\text{CCR} < 0$ (shared bias). CCR correlates with combo gain: Spearman $\rho = 0.839$, 95\% bootstrap CI $[0.338, 0.990]$; LOO-robust (min $p = 0.014$). Four of eight conditions share the DeBERTa verifier, yielding effective $n < 10$; results should be interpreted as a consistent pattern rather than a precise effect size.
215

217	\centering
218	\small

Core insight: constraints are a lossy function\n      of traces that already contain the answer. BR > 1 follows from the\n      definition of majority voting. No assumptions about model capability.\n\n  B_cil:\n    priority: high\n    placement: \"§3.2 (definition) + §5/§6 (empirical)\"\n    space_cost: \"~4 lines + 1 equation = ~0.08 pages\"\n    definition: |\n      CIL(V, G) = I(V_output; GT | G_answer)\n      \"How much does the verifier add beyond the generator's answer?\"\n    theoretical_predictions:\n      self_extraction: >\n        CIL ≈ 0. By the causal DAG (D), C ⊥ GT | A, so self-extracted\n        constraints contain no information about ground truth beyond\n        the generator's answer. This is the information-theoretic\n        restatement of SCB.\n      cross_architecture: >\n        CIL > 0 when V has an independent information path to GT\n        (i.e., V does not share M's distributional prior).\n    operationalization: >\n      CIL is the theoretical ideal. We operationalize it through the\n      Conditional Correction Ratio (CCR; Appendix D):\n      CCR(V,G) = log[P(V correct | G wrong) / P(V correct | G correct)]\n      CCR > 0 ↔ CIL > 0 (verifier preferentially corrects errors).\n      Empirical CCR correlates with combo gain: ρ = 0.84, p = 0.014.\n    data_needs: >\n      No new computation needed. Existing CCR data (8 conditions in\n      Appendix D) serves as the empirical proxy. The d-separation\n      argument gives CIL ≈ 0 for self-extraction theoretically.\n      OPTIONAL: Agent could compute actual MI values from per-problem\n      (G_answer, V_output, GT) tuples in combo data for a cleaner\n      presentation. Flag as nice-to-have, not blocking.\n\n  C_capability_gap:\n    priority: medium\n    placement: \"§6.1 (before empirical results)\"\n    space_cost: \"~5 lines + 1 equation = ~0.10 pages\"\n    derivation: |\n      Under conditional independence between G (accuracy SC_G) and V\n      (accuracy V_acc):\n      ΔCombo ≈ (1 - SC_G) · V_acc - SC_G · (1 - V_acc)\n             = V_acc - SC_G\n      Properties:\n      - Monotonically decreasing in SC_G ✓\n      - ΔCombo = 0 when SC_G = V_acc (breakeven at capability parity) ✓\n      - ΔCombo > 0 when SC_G < V_acc (verifier stronger) ✓\n      - As SC_G → 1, gain → 0 (ceiling: few errors to correct) ✓\n    empirical_validation: >\n      DeBERTa standalone V_acc = 55%. Model predicts breakeven at\n      SC_G ≈ 55%. Empirically: Mistral (SC 39%) gains (+7pp),\n      all generators above 72% show no significant gain.\n      Spearman ρ = 1.0, p = 0.003, n=6 matches the monotonic prediction.\n      Magnitude attenuated vs linear model due to imperfect independence\n      and vote-weighting mechanics — state this as expected.\n    note: >\n      This is a first-order approximation. State it as such:\n      \"Under a simplified independence model...\" with the caveat that\n      real combo voting involves weighted margins.\n\n  D_causal_dag:\n    priority: highest\n    placement: \"§3.2 (The Information Bottleneck) — as Figure\"\n    space_cost: \"~0.20 pages (TikZ figure, single-column)\"\n    dag_structure: |\n      Self-extraction DAG:\n        Q → GT (ground truth)\n        Q, M → T (trace)\n        T → A (answer, contained in trace)\n        T, M → C (constraint, extracted by same model)\n        {A, C} → S (MAX-SAT score)\n\n      d-separation: C ⊥ GT | A\n      All paths from C to GT: C ← (T,M) ← Q → GT\n      Conditioning on A (which is T → A) blocks the chain.\n      Interpretation: constraints carry no information about GT\n      beyond what's already in the answer.\n    cross_arch_contrast: |\n      Cross-architecture DAG:\n        Q → GT\n        Q, M_G → T → A → S\n        Q, M_V → V_out → S (independent path)\n\n      V_out ⊥̸ GT | A because V has an independent path Q → M_V → V_out\n      that doesn't pass through A. Hence CIL > 0.\n    figure_design: >\n      Two-panel TikZ figure (a) Self-extraction (b) Cross-architecture.\n      Panel (a): show A as an information bottleneck node (highlighted).\n      Panel (b): show V_out with independent path to GT (highlighted).\n      Compact, ~0.5 column width per panel. Breaks pages 3-4 text wall.\n      Gray/blue color scheme matching Figure 1.\n\n# ──────────────────────────────────────────────\n# NEW SECTION STRUCTURE\n# ──────────────────────────────────────────────"
}

State it as such:\n      \"Under a simplified independence model...\" with the caveat that\n      real combo voting involves weighted margins.\n\n  D_causal_dag:\n    priority: highest\n    placement: \"§3.2 (The Information Bottleneck) — as Figure\"\n    space_cost: \"~0.20 pages (TikZ figure, single-column)\"\n    dag_structure: |\n      Self-extraction DAG:\n        Q → GT (ground truth)\n        Q, M → T (trace)\n        T → A (answer, contained in trace)\n        T, M → C (constraint, extracted by same model)\n        {A, C} → S (MAX-SAT score)\n\n      d-separation: C ⊥ GT | A\n      All paths from C to GT: C ← (T,M) ← Q → GT\n      Conditioning on A (which is T → A) blocks the chain.\n      Interpretation: constraints carry no information about GT\n      beyond what's already in the answer.\n    cross_arch_contrast: |\n      Cross-architecture DAG:\n        Q → GT\n        Q, M_G → T → A → S\n        Q, M_V → V_out → S (independent path)\n\n      V_out ⊥̸ GT | A because V has an independent path Q → M_V → V_out\n      that doesn't pass through A. Hence CIL > 0.\n    figure_design: >\n      Two-panel TikZ figure (a) Self-extraction (b) Cross-architecture.\n      Panel (a): show A as an information bottleneck node (highlighted).\n      Panel (b): show V_out with independent path to GT (highlighted).\n      Compact, ~0.5 column width per panel. Breaks pages 3-4 text wall.\n      Gray/blue color scheme matching Figure 1.\n\n# ──────────────────────────────────────────────\n# NEW SECTION STRUCTURE\n# ──────────────────────────────────────────────"
  }
}

_source line 13507 · 2026-05-23T01:09:59.625464+00:00_

[Director 指令] writing_plan.yaml V4 审批结果：**条件性批准**，需追加 ABCD 理论深度整合。

## 批准部分
restructure_rationale 7 个问题诊断 + 解法全部批准。section 重组、缩写精简、display item 重分配、CJT 压缩、Mirage 后移到 §5 的 data-first 叙事——方向正确。execution_order steps 1-11 的基础流程批准。

## 标题决策
保留当前标题 "Beyond Self-Consistency: Why Self-Extracted Constraints Fail to Improve LLM Logical Reasoning"。备选标题虽更抓人但 "Adds No Information" 在 power < 21% 下同样可能被审稿人挑战。

## 必须追加：ABCD 理论深度
用户明确指示"ABCD全做"，当前 plan 只做了 presentation 改善，缺少理论贡献升级。Round 15 审稿核心问题是"三项声称的概念贡献均有先验对应"——presentation 改善能拿到 3.5→3.75，但 4.0 需要原创理论深度。以下四项必须整合进 execution_order：

### A. SCB 概率证明 → 插入 §5.1 SCB 小节
- 写一个 Proposition 1（不用 tcolorbox，用 \textbf{Proposition 1.} 格式）
- 内容：构建概率模型（M 生成 trace T 含答案 a_M → 提取约束 c 是 T 的有损函数 → c 继承 a_M 的分布先验 → E[BR] ≥ 1）
- 关键洞察：约束的信息来源就是 trace，trace 已包含答案，所以约束不可能提供独立于答案的新信息
- 目标：3-5 行的 clean 形式化论证，把 SCB 从"经验观察"升级为"理论必然"
- 直接回应 W2（SCB vs CoT bias 区分）——我们的贡献是证明这在 formal verification 场景下有信息论证明

### B. CIL 信息论指标 → 插入 §5.4 诊断框架（或 §6 escape）
- 定义 CIL(V, G) = I(V_output; ground_truth | G_answer)
- Self-extraction: CIL ≈ 0（约束不含超出答案的信息）
- Cross-architecture: CIL > 0（独立模型带新信息）
- Agent 正在计算所有条件的 CIL 数据，计算完成后补入
- 如果 CIL 精确预测 combo gain 方向，这统一了 K_eff + BR + C₀ 三个 ad hoc 指标
- 直接回应 W5（K_eff 新颖性不足）——CIL 是原创的信息论贡献

### C. Capability Gap 理论模型 → 插入 §6 escape
- 1 段理论推导：combo gain ∝ (1 - SC_G) × V_acc × (1 - ρ_{GV})
- 当 SC_G → 1 时 gain → 0（天花板效应）
- 当 SC_G 很低时 gain 被 V_acc 上界控制
- 这解释了为什么 gain 随 SC 单调递减（不再只是 n=6 经验曲线）
- 直接回应 W13（n=6 脆弱）——有理论支撑的 n=6 比纯经验的 n=6 强得多

### D. 因果 DAG (M→T→A→C→S) → 插入 §5 作为第二个 TikZ 图
- 与 diagnostic decision tree 共存：DAG 解释 WHY（因果结构），decision tree 解释 HOW（实操检查）
- 证明 C 在给定 A 条件下与 ground truth 条件独立（d-separation）
- 一张图直观解释"约束的所有信息都经过答案这个瓶颈"
- 如果 §5 空间不够放两个 TikZ，把 DAG 和 Proposition 1 做成一个紧凑的 1/3 页组合

## 修改后的 execution_order
在 step 1（diagnostic tree）之后、step 2 之前，插入：
- step 1.5: 写 SCB Proposition 1 + 因果 DAG TikZ（Directions A+D）
- step 4 的 analysis.tex rewrite 中，在 diagnostics 小节追加 CIL 定义和数据（Direction B，数据到了再补）
- step 5 的 solutions.tex rewrite 中，追加 capability gap 理论模型段落（Direction C）

## 数据一致性确认
solutions.tex 应保持 SC=39.33%, +6.67pp（Table 1 SICA pipeline 对齐值）。Table 3 的 SC=39.17% 是 combo eval 对齐，两者语境不同，都正确。introduction.tex line 28 的 +6.67pp 也保持不变。

## 质量要求
用户明确说"质量 > 速度"。ABCD 理论部分的数学必须严谨——特别是 Proposition 1 的证明，要经得住审稿人推敲。如果某个方向写不好，宁可不写也不能写成花架子。

请更新 writing_plan.yaml 整合以上要求后开始执行。
